- **conbio-conservation-relevance-and-implications**: Use when sharpening the conservation significance and actionable implications of a Conservation Biology manuscript. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Conservation-Biology-Skills/skills/conbio-data-analysis) · ★1.1k)
